  refine Step.seqCons (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (V1 := (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (("q_ptr", BitVec.ofNat 256 p) :: V) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.byteU32Bytes)) "q_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 v))) (st1 := (touchMemory (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (BitVec.ofNat 256 p).toNat 32)) s39 ?_
  exact Step.seqCons (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (V1 := (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (("q_ptr", BitVec.ofNat 256 p) :: V) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.byteU32Bytes)) "q_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 v)) "q_has_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 1))) (st1 := (touchMemory (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (BitVec.ofNat 256 p).toNat 32)) s46 (Step.seqNil (D := Bridge.Dialect c g))

theorem qArmValue_pushMemLiteral {c : ExternalCalls} {g : ExternalGas}
    (ctx : PartialCtx) (program : QuotientProgram) (tcq : QuotientVmTemplateConstants)
    (funs : FunEnv (Bridge.Dialect c g)) (pc sp x : U256) (p v : Nat)
    :
    StmtsContract (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (GenAst.qArmBodyPushMemLiteral ctx program tcq)
      (fun V st => VEnv.get V "q_pc" = some pc ∧
        VEnv.get V "q_sp" = some sp ∧
        VEnv.get V "q_has_top" = some 0 ∧
        VEnv.get V "q_top" = some x ∧
        ((loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) >>> 224) = BitVec.ofNat 256 p ∧
        loadWord st.memory (BitVec.ofNat 256 p).toNat = BitVec.ofNat 256 v)
      (fun V st V' st' o => o = .normal 
        ∧ st' = (touchMemory (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (BitVec.ofNat 256 p).toNat 32)
        ∧ V' = (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (("q_ptr", BitVec.ofNat 256 p) :: V) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.byteU32Bytes)) "q_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 v)) "q_has_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 1))) := by
  intro V st hpre
  have hrun := qArmRun_pushMemLiteral ctx program tcq funs V st pc sp x p v hpre.1 hpre.2.1 hpre.2.2.1 hpre.2.2.2.1 hpre.2.2.2.2.1 hpre.2.2.2.2.2
  exact ⟨_, _, _, hrun, ⟨rfl, rfl, rfl⟩⟩

theorem qArmValueAll_pushMemLiteral {c : ExternalCalls} {g : ExternalGas}
    (hc : c.Functional) (hg : g.Functional)
    (ctx : PartialCtx) (program : QuotientProgram) (tcq : QuotientVmTemplateConstants)
    (funs : FunEnv (Bridge.Dialect c g)) (pc sp x : U256) (p v : Nat)
    :
    StmtsContractAll (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (GenAst.qArmBodyPushMemLiteral ctx program tcq)
      (fun V st => VEnv.get V "q_pc" = some pc ∧
        VEnv.get V "q_sp" = some sp ∧
        VEnv.get V "q_has_top" = some 0 ∧
        VEnv.get V "q_top" = some x ∧
        ((loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) >>> 224) = BitVec.ofNat 256 p ∧
        loadWord st.memory (BitVec.ofNat 256 p).toNat = BitVec.ofNat 256 v)
      (fun V st V' st' o => o = .normal 
        ∧ st' = (touchMemory (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (BitVec.ofNat 256 p).toNat 32)
        ∧ V' = (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (("q_ptr", BitVec.ofNat 256 p) :: V) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.byteU32Bytes)) "q_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 v)) "q_has_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 1))) :=
  StmtsContractAll.of_det (Bridge.dialect_deterministic hc hg)
    (qArmValue_pushMemLiteral ctx program tcq funs pc sp x p v )

theorem qArmRun_pushConstU8 {c : ExternalCalls} {g : ExternalGas}
    (ctx : PartialCtx) (program : QuotientProgram) (tcq : QuotientVmTemplateConstants)
    (funs : FunEnv (Bridge.Dialect c g)) (V : VEnv (Bridge.Dialect c g)) (st : EvmState)
    (pc cb sp x : U256) (b v : Nat)
    (hget_pc : VEnv.get V "q_pc" = some pc)
    (hget_sp : VEnv.get V "q_sp" = some sp)
    (hget_ht : VEnv.get V "q_has_top" = some 0)
    (hget_top : VEnv.get V "q_top" = some x)
    (hget_cb : VEnv.get V "q_const_mptr" = some cb)
    (hdec : (((loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) >>> 248) &&& 0xff) = BitVec.ofNat 256 b)
    (hconst : loadWord st.memory (cb + ((BitVec.ofNat 256 b) <<< 5)).toNat = BitVec.ofNat 256 v) :
    ExecStmts (Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (GenAst.qArmBodyPushConstU8 ctx program tcq)
      (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) "q_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 v)) "q_has_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 1)) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1)) (touchMemory (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (cb + ((BitVec.ofNat 256 b) <<< 5)).toNat 32) .normal := by
  have a1 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (.args []) (.eres (.vals [] st)) :=
    Step.argsNil (D := Bridge.Dialect c g)
  have a2 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (.args []) (.eres (.vals [] st)) :=
    Step.argsNil (D := Bridge.Dialect c g)
  have x3 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (.expr (yvar "q_pc")) (.eres (.vals [pc] st)) :=
    Step.var (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) hget_pc
  have a4 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (.args [yvar "q_pc"]) (.eres (.vals [pc] st)) :=
    Step.argsCons (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (rest := []) (restvals := []) (st1 := st) (v := pc) (st2 := st) a2 x3
  have x5 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (.expr (yop Op.mload [yvar "q_pc"])) (.eres (.vals [loadWord st.memory pc.toNat] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.builtinOk a4 (qbi_mload_at st st.memory pc rfl)
  have w6 :
    (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32).memory = st.memory :=
    Midfall.Yul.Word.touchMemory_memory st pc.toNat 32
  have a7 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (.args [yop Op.mload [yvar "q_pc"]]) (.eres (.vals [loadWord st.memory pc.toNat] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.argsCons (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (rest := []) (restvals := []) (st1 := st) (v := loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) (st2 := touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) a1 x5
  have x8 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (.expr (ylit 0)) (.eres (.vals [BitVec.ofNat 256 0] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.lit (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (l := Literal.number 0)
  have a9 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (.args [ylit 0, yop Op.mload [yvar "q_pc"]]) (.eres (.vals [BitVec.ofNat 256 0, loadWord st.memory pc.toNat] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.argsCons (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (rest := [yop Op.mload [yvar "q_pc"]]) (restvals := [loadWord st.memory pc.toNat]) (st1 := touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (v := BitVec.ofNat 256 0) (st2 := touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) a7 x8
  have x10 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (.expr (yop Op.byte [ylit 0, yop Op.mload [yvar "q_pc"]])) (.eres (.vals [(((loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) >>> 248) &&& 0xff)] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.builtinOk a9 (qbi_byte0 (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (loadWord st.memory pc.toNat))
  have d11 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (.expr (yop Op.byte [ylit 0, yop Op.mload [yvar "q_pc"]])) (.eres (.vals [BitVec.ofNat 256 b] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=

    venvGet_set_ne (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) "q_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 v)) "q_has_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 1)) "q_pc" "q_top" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1) (BitVec.ofNat 256 v) (by decide : "q_pc" ≠ "q_top") g50
  have g64 :
    VEnv.get (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) "q_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 v)) "q_has_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 1)) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1)) "qconst" = some (BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :=
    venvGet_set_ne (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) "q_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 v)) "q_has_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 1)) "q_pc" "qconst" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1) (BitVec.ofNat 256 b) (by decide : "q_pc" ≠ "qconst") g51
  have g65 :
    VEnv.get (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) "q_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 v)) "q_has_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 1)) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1)) "q_pc" = some (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1) :=
    venvGet_set_same (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) "q_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 v)) "q_has_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 1)) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1) pc g48
  unfold GenAst.qArmBodyPushConstU8
  refine Step.seqCons (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (V1 := (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V)) (st1 := (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32)) s12 ?_
  refine Step.seqCons (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (V1 := (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V)) (st1 := (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32)) s21 ?_
  refine Step.seqCons (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (V1 := (VEnv.set (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) "q_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 v))) (st1 := (touchMemory (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (cb + ((BitVec.ofNat 256 b) <<< 5)).toNat 32)) s38 ?_
  refine Step.seqCons (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (V1 := (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) "q_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 v)) "q_has_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 1))) (st1 := (touchMemory (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (cb + ((BitVec.ofNat 256 b) <<< 5)).toNat 32)) s46 ?_
  exact Step.seqCons (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (V1 := (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) "q_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 v)) "q_has_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 1)) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1))) (st1 := (touchMemory (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (cb + ((BitVec.ofNat 256 b) <<< 5)).toNat 32)) s59 (Step.seqNil (D := Bridge.Dialect c g))

theorem qArmValue_pushConstU8 {c : ExternalCalls} {g : ExternalGas}
    (ctx : PartialCtx) (program : QuotientProgram) (tcq : QuotientVmTemplateConstants)
    (funs : FunEnv (Bridge.Dialect c g)) (pc cb sp x : U256) (b v : Nat)
    :
    StmtsContract (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (GenAst.qArmBodyPushConstU8 ctx program tcq)
      (fun V st => VEnv.get V "q_pc" = some pc ∧
        VEnv.get V "q_sp" = some sp ∧
        VEnv.get V "q_has_top" = some 0 ∧
        VEnv.get V "q_top" = some x ∧
        VEnv.get V "q_const_mptr" = some cb ∧
        (((loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) >>> 248) &&& 0xff) = BitVec.ofNat 256 b ∧
        loadWord st.memory (cb + ((BitVec.ofNat 256 b) <<< 5)).toNat = BitVec.ofNat 256 v)
      (fun V st V' st' o => o = .normal 
        ∧ st' = (touchMemory (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (cb + ((BitVec.ofNat 256 b) <<< 5)).toNat 32)
        ∧ V' = (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) "q_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 v)) "q_has_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 1)) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1))) := by
  intro V st hpre
  have hrun := qArmRun_pushConstU8 ctx program tcq funs V st pc cb sp x b v hpre.1 hpre.2.1 hpre.2.2.1 hpre.2.2.2.1 hpre.2.2.2.2.1 hpre.2.2.2.2.2.1 hpre.2.2.2.2.2.2
  exact ⟨_, _, _, hrun, ⟨rfl, rfl, rfl⟩⟩

theorem qArmValueAll_pushConstU8 {c : ExternalCalls} {g : ExternalGas}
    (hc : c.Functional) (hg : g.Functional)
    (ctx : PartialCtx) (program : QuotientProgram) (tcq : QuotientVmTemplateConstants)
    (funs : FunEnv (Bridge.Dialect c g)) (pc cb sp x : U256) (b v : Nat)
    :
    StmtsContractAll (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (GenAst.qArmBodyPushConstU8 ctx program tcq)
      (fun V st => VEnv.get V "q_pc" = some pc ∧
        VEnv.get V "q_sp" = some sp ∧
        VEnv.get V "q_has_top" = some 0 ∧
        VEnv.get V "q_top" = some x ∧
        VEnv.get V "q_const_mptr" = some cb ∧
        (((loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) >>> 248) &&& 0xff) = BitVec.ofNat 256 b ∧
        loadWord st.memory (cb + ((BitVec.ofNat 256 b) <<< 5)).toNat = BitVec.ofNat 256 v)
      (fun V st V' st' o => o = .normal 
        ∧ st' = (touchMemory (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (cb + ((BitVec.ofNat 256 b) <<< 5)).toNat 32)
        ∧ V' = (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) "q_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 v)) "q_has_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 1)) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1))) :=
  StmtsContractAll.of_det (Bridge.dialect_deterministic hc hg)
    (qArmValue_pushConstU8 ctx program tcq funs pc cb sp x b v )

theorem qArmRun_pushConst {c : ExternalCalls} {g : ExternalGas}
    (ctx : PartialCtx) (program : QuotientProgram) (tcq : QuotientVmTemplateConstants)
    (funs : FunEnv (Bridge.Dialect c g)) (V : VEnv (Bridge.Dialect c g)) (st : EvmState)
    (pc cb sp x : U256) (b v : Nat)
    (hget_pc : VEnv.get V "q_pc" = some pc)
    (hget_sp : VEnv.get V "q_sp" = some sp)
    (hget_ht : VEnv.get V "q_has_top" = some 0)
    (hget_top : VEnv.get V "q_top" = some x)
    (hget_cb : VEnv.get V "q_const_mptr" = some cb)
    (hdec : ((loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) >>> 240) = BitVec.ofNat 256 b)
    (hconst : loadWord st.memory (cb + ((BitVec.ofNat 256 b) <<< 5)).toNat = BitVec.ofNat 256 v)
    (hguard : (BitVec.ofNat 256 b).toNat ≤ (BitVec.ofNat 256 program.guards.constMax).toNat) :
    ExecStmts (Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (GenAst.qArmBodyPushConst ctx program tcq)
      (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) "q_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 v)) "q_has_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 1)) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 2)) (touchMemory (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (cb + ((BitVec.ofNat 256 b) <<< 5)).toNat 32) .normal := by
  have a1 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (.args []) (.eres (.vals [] st)) :=
    Step.argsNil (D := Bridge.Dialect c g)
  have a2 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (.args []) (.eres (.vals [] st)) :=
    Step.argsNil (D := Bridge.Dialect c g)
  have x3 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (.expr (yvar "q_pc")) (.eres (.vals [pc] st)) :=
    Step.var (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) hget_pc
  have a4 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (.args [yvar "q_pc"]) (.eres (.vals [pc] st)) :=
    Step.argsCons (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (rest := []) (restvals := []) (st1 := st) (v := pc) (st2 := st) a2 x3
  have x5 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (.expr (yop Op.mload [yvar "q_pc"])) (.eres (.vals [loadWord st.memory pc.toNat] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.builtinOk a4 (qbi_mload_at st st.memory pc rfl)
  have w6 :
    (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32).memory = st.memory :=
    Midfall.Yul.Word.touchMemory_memory st pc.toNat 32
  have a7 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (.args [yop Op.mload [yvar "q_pc"]]) (.eres (.vals [loadWord st.memory pc.toNat] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.argsCons (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (rest := []) (restvals := []) (st1 := st) (v := loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) (st2 := touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) a1 x5
  have x8 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (.expr (ylit 240)) (.eres (.vals [BitVec.ofNat 256 240] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.lit (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (l := Literal.number 240)
  have a9 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (.args [ylit 240, yop Op.mload [yvar "q_pc"]]) (.eres (.vals [BitVec.ofNat 256 240, loadWord st.memory pc.toNat] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.argsCons (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (rest := [yop Op.mload [yvar "q_pc"]]) (restvals := [loadWord st.memory pc.toNat]) (st1 := touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (v := BitVec.ofNat 256 240) (st2 := touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) a7 x8
  have x10 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (.expr (yop Op.shr [ylit 240, yop Op.mload [yvar "q_pc"]])) (.eres (.vals [((loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) >>> 240)] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.builtinOk a9 (qbi_shr240 (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (loadWord st.memory pc.toNat))
  have d11 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (.expr (yop Op.shr [ylit 240, yop Op.mload [yvar "q_pc"]])) (.eres (.vals [BitVec.ofNat 256 b] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    by rw [<- hdec]; exact x10
  have s12 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (.stmt (ylet "qconst" (yop Op.shr [ylit 240, yop Op.mload [yvar "q_pc"]]))) (.sres (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) .normal) :=
    Step.letVal d11 rfl
  have g13 :
    VEnv.get (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) "q_const_mptr" = some cb :=
    by rw [venvGet_cons_ne V "qconst" "q_const_mptr" (BitVec.ofNat 256 b) (by decide : "qconst" ≠ "q_const_mptr")]; exact hget_cb
  have g14 :
    VEnv.get (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) "q_has_top" = some 0 :=
    by rw [venvGet_cons_ne V "qconst" "q_has_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 b) (by decide : "qconst" ≠ "q_has_top")]; exact hget_ht
  have g15 :
    VEnv.get (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) "q_pc" = some pc :=
    by rw [venvGet_cons_ne V "qconst" "q_pc" (BitVec.ofNat 256 b) (by decide : "qconst" ≠ "q_pc")]; exact hget_pc
  have g16 :
    VEnv.get (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) "q_sp" = some sp :=
    by rw [venvGet_cons_ne V "qconst" "q_sp" (BitVec.ofNat 256 b) (by decide : "qconst" ≠ "q_sp")]; exact hget_sp
  have g17 :
    VEnv.get (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) "q_top" = some x :=
    by rw [venvGet_cons_ne V "qconst" "q_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 b) (by decide : "qconst" ≠ "q_top")]; exact hget_top
  have g18 :
    VEnv.get (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) "qconst" = some (BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :=
    venvGet_cons_eq V "qconst" (BitVec.ofNat 256 b)
  have a19 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (.args []) (.eres (.vals [] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.argsNil (D := Bridge.Dialect c g)
  have x20 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (.expr (ylit program.guards.constMax)) (.eres (.vals [BitVec.ofNat 256 program.guards.constMax] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.lit (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (l := Literal.number program.guards.constMax)
  have a21 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (.args [ylit program.guards.constMax]) (.eres (.vals [BitVec.ofNat 256 program.guards.constMax] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.argsCons (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (rest := []) (restvals := []) (st1 := touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (v := BitVec.ofNat 256 program.guards.constMax) (st2 := touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) a19 x20
  have x22 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (.expr (yvar "qconst")) (.eres (.vals [BitVec.ofNat 256 b] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.var (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) g18
  have a23 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (.args [yvar "qconst", ylit program.guards.constMax]) (.eres (.vals [BitVec.ofNat 256 b, BitVec.ofNat 256 program.guards.constMax] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.argsCons (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (rest := [ylit program.guards.constMax]) (restvals := [BitVec.ofNat 256 program.guards.constMax]) (st1 := touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (v := BitVec.ofNat 256 b) (st2 := touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) a21 x22
  have x24 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (.expr (yop Op.gt [yvar "qconst", ylit program.guards.constMax])) (.eres (.vals [b2w (decide ((BitVec.ofNat 256 program.guards.constMax).toNat < (BitVec.ofNat 256 b).toNat))] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.builtinOk a23 (qbi_gt (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (BitVec.ofNat 256 b) (BitVec.ofNat 256 program.guards.constMax))
  have z25 :
    b2w (decide ((BitVec.ofNat 256 program.guards.constMax).toNat < (BitVec.ofNat 256 b).toNat)) = (Bridge.Dialect c g).zero :=
    by rw [decide_eq_false (by omega : ¬ ((BitVec.ofNat 256 program.guards.constMax).toNat < (BitVec.ofNat 256 b).toNat))]; rfl
  have s26 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (.stmt (yif (yop Op.gt [yvar "qconst", ylit program.guards.constMax]) [yexpr (ycall "q_program_fail" [])])) (.sres (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) .normal) :=
    Step.ifFalse x24 z25
  have x27 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (.expr (yvar "q_has_top")) (.eres (.vals [0] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.var (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) g14
  have z28 : 0 = (Bridge.Dialect c g).zero := rfl
  have s29 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (.stmt (yif (yvar "q_has_top") [yif (yop Op.gt [yvar "q_sp", ylit program.guards.stackLast]) [yexpr (ycall "q_program_fail" [])], yexpr (yop Op.mstore [yvar "q_sp", yvar "q_top"]), yset "q_sp" (yop Op.add [yvar "q_sp", ylit 32])])) (.sres (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) .normal) :=
    Step.ifFalse x27 z28
  have a30 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (.args []) (.eres (.vals [] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.argsNil (D := Bridge.Dialect c g)
  have a31 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (.args []) (.eres (.vals [] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.argsNil (D := Bridge.Dialect c g)
  have a32 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (.args []) (.eres (.vals [] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.argsNil (D := Bridge.Dialect c g)
  have x33 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (.expr (yvar "qconst")) (.eres (.vals [BitVec.ofNat 256 b] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.var (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) g18
  have a34 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (.args [yvar "qconst"]) (.eres (.vals [BitVec.ofNat 256 b] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.argsCons (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (rest := []) (restvals := []) (st1 := touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (v := BitVec.ofNat 256 b) (st2 := touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) a32 x33
  have x35 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (.expr (ylit 5)) (.eres (.vals [BitVec.ofNat 256 5] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.lit (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (l := Literal.number 5)
  have a36 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (.args [ylit 5, yvar "qconst"]) (.eres (.vals [BitVec.ofNat 256 5, BitVec.ofNat 256 b] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.argsCons (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (rest := [yvar "qconst"]) (restvals := [BitVec.ofNat 256 b]) (st1 := touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (v := BitVec.ofNat 256 5) (st2 := touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) a34 x35
  have x37 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (.expr (yop Op.shl [ylit 5, yvar "qconst"])) (.eres (.vals [((BitVec.ofNat 256 b) <<< 5)] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.builtinOk a36 (qbi_shl5 (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (BitVec.ofNat 256 b))
  have a38 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (.args [yop Op.shl [ylit 5, yvar "qconst"]]) (.eres (.vals [((BitVec.ofNat 256 b) <<< 5)] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.argsCons (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (rest := []) (restvals := []) (st1 := touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (v := ((BitVec.ofNat 256 b) <<< 5)) (st2 := touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) a31 x37
  have x39 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (.expr (yvar "q_const_mptr")) (.eres (.vals [cb] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.var (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) g13
  have a40 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (.args [yvar "q_const_mptr", yop Op.shl [ylit 5, yvar "qconst"]]) (.eres (.vals [cb, ((BitVec.ofNat 256 b) <<< 5)] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.argsCons (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (rest := [yop Op.shl [ylit 5, yvar "qconst"]]) (restvals := [((BitVec.ofNat 256 b) <<< 5)]) (st1 := touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (v := cb) (st2 := touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) a38 x39
  have x41 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (.expr (yop Op.add [yvar "q_const_mptr", yop Op.shl [ylit 5, yvar "qconst"]])) (.eres (.vals [(cb + ((BitVec.ofNat 256 b) <<< 5))] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.builtinOk a40 (qbi_add (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) cb ((BitVec.ofNat 256 b) <<< 5))
  have a42 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (.args [yop Op.add [yvar "q_const_mptr", yop Op.shl [ylit 5, yvar "qconst"]]]) (.eres (.vals [(cb + ((BitVec.ofNat 256 b) <<< 5))] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.argsCons (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (rest := []) (restvals := []) (st1 := touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (v := (cb + ((BitVec.ofNat 256 b) <<< 5))) (st2 := touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) a30 x41
  have x43 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (.expr (yop Op.mload [yop Op.add [yvar "q_const_mptr", yop Op.shl [ylit 5, yvar "qconst"]]])) (.eres (.vals [loadWord st.memory (cb + ((BitVec.ofNat 256 b) <<< 5)).toNat] (touchMemory (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (cb + ((BitVec.ofNat 256 b) <<< 5)).toNat 32))) :=
    Step.builtinOk a42 (qbi_mload_at (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) st.memory (cb + ((BitVec.ofNat 256 b) <<< 5)) w6)
  have d44 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (.expr (yop Op.mload [yop Op.add [yvar "q_const_mptr", yop Op.shl [ylit 5, yvar "qconst"]]])) (.eres (.vals [BitVec.ofNat 256 v] (touchMemory (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (cb + ((BitVec.ofNat 256 b) <<< 5)).toNat 32))) :=
    by rw [<- hconst]; exact x43
  have w45 :
    (touchMemory (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (cb + ((BitVec.ofNat 256 b) <<< 5)).toNat 32).memory = st.memory :=
    Eq.trans (Midfall.Yul.Word.touchMemory_memory (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (cb + ((BitVec.ofNat 256 b) <<< 5)).toNat 32) w6
  have s46 :

theorem qArmValue_pushConst {c : ExternalCalls} {g : ExternalGas}
    (ctx : PartialCtx) (program : QuotientProgram) (tcq : QuotientVmTemplateConstants)
    (funs : FunEnv (Bridge.Dialect c g)) (pc cb sp x : U256) (b v : Nat)
    (hguard : (BitVec.ofNat 256 b).toNat ≤ (BitVec.ofNat 256 program.guards.constMax).toNat)
    :
    StmtsContract (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (GenAst.qArmBodyPushConst ctx program tcq)
      (fun V st => VEnv.get V "q_pc" = some pc ∧
        VEnv.get V "q_sp" = some sp ∧
        VEnv.get V "q_has_top" = some 0 ∧
        VEnv.get V "q_top" = some x ∧
        VEnv.get V "q_const_mptr" = some cb ∧
        ((loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) >>> 240) = BitVec.ofNat 256 b ∧
        loadWord st.memory (cb + ((BitVec.ofNat 256 b) <<< 5)).toNat = BitVec.ofNat 256 v)
      (fun V st V' st' o => o = .normal 
        ∧ st' = (touchMemory (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (cb + ((BitVec.ofNat 256 b) <<< 5)).toNat 32)
        ∧ V' = (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) "q_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 v)) "q_has_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 1)) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 2))) := by
  intro V st hpre
  have hrun := qArmRun_pushConst ctx program tcq funs V st pc cb sp x b v hpre.1 hpre.2.1 hpre.2.2.1 hpre.2.2.2.1 hpre.2.2.2.2.1 hpre.2.2.2.2.2.1 hpre.2.2.2.2.2.2 hguard
  exact ⟨_, _, _, hrun, ⟨rfl, rfl, rfl⟩⟩

theorem qArmValueAll_pushConst {c : ExternalCalls} {g : ExternalGas}
    (hc : c.Functional) (hg : g.Functional)
    (ctx : PartialCtx) (program : QuotientProgram) (tcq : QuotientVmTemplateConstants)
    (funs : FunEnv (Bridge.Dialect c g)) (pc cb sp x : U256) (b v : Nat)
    (hguard : (BitVec.ofNat 256 b).toNat ≤ (BitVec.ofNat 256 program.guards.constMax).toNat)
    :
    StmtsContractAll (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (GenAst.qArmBodyPushConst ctx program tcq)
      (fun V st => VEnv.get V "q_pc" = some pc ∧
        VEnv.get V "q_sp" = some sp ∧
        VEnv.get V "q_has_top" = some 0 ∧
        VEnv.get V "q_top" = some x ∧
        VEnv.get V "q_const_mptr" = some cb ∧
        ((loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) >>> 240) = BitVec.ofNat 256 b ∧
        loadWord st.memory (cb + ((BitVec.ofNat 256 b) <<< 5)).toNat = BitVec.ofNat 256 v)
      (fun V st V' st' o => o = .normal 
        ∧ st' = (touchMemory (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (cb + ((BitVec.ofNat 256 b) <<< 5)).toNat 32)
        ∧ V' = (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (("qconst", BitVec.ofNat 256 b) :: V) "q_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 v)) "q_has_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 1)) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 2))) :=
  StmtsContractAll.of_det (Bridge.dialect_deterministic hc hg)
    (qArmValue_pushConst ctx program tcq funs pc cb sp x b v hguard)

/-- **QTOKEN - THE TWO SWITCH-GATED TOKEN ARMS.** These are the first arms in the file whose emitted
    body contains a 'Stmt.switch', and they needed three things QMEM named but could not use: the flag
    of the case taken ('hl0 : program.memUsage.l0 = true' - 'List.find?' short-circuits on the head, so
    the other eight memUsage flags are irrelevant), the scrutinee pinned to that case's own label
    ('hbyte' below, discharged by 'decide_eq_true (Eq.refl _)' so no 'DecidableEq' computation on a
    symbolic 'tcq.mem.l0' is ever demanded), and 'restore_set' to turn 'Step.block'\'s
    'restore V Vb' back into the 'VEnv.set' form the contract states. All three are in 'QuotientFrame'.

    STATE EQUATION, per arm. 'pushMemToken': 'touch(pc)' by the 'byte 0 (mload q_pc)' decode, then
    'touch(L_0_MPTR)' by the pushed read - 'touchMemory (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32)
    (BitVec.ofNat 256 p).toNat 32', with 'q_pc' advanced by ONE (the operand byte; the reference's
    'consumed := 2' counts the opcode too). 'pushMemTokenOffset': same two touches but the second is at
    '(BitVec.ofNat 256 p + BitVec.ofNat 256 ow).toNat' - the u32 offset read from bytes 1..4 of the word
    at 'q_pc' - and 'q_pc' advances by FIVE. Neither is the uniform 'st' = 'st' of the register-only arms.

    CANONICITY: NEITHER arm needs it. No field operation appears in either body at all - both end in
    'q_top := mload q_ptr', so the pushed word is a raw memory read and there is nothing to reduce mod
    'qvmFrModulus'. That is a fact about these two arms, not a general exemption.

    PRECONDITIONS: 'q_pc', 'q_sp', 'q_top', 'q_has_top = 0' and 'L_0_MPTR' pinned by 'VEnv.get', the
    token byte and the pushed word pinned at the EMITTED expressions, and 'q_has_top = 0' so the spill
    branch is not taken (a one-instruction identity segment starts with the reference stack '[]'). -/
theorem qArmRun_pushMemToken {c : ExternalCalls} {g : ExternalGas}
    (ctx : PartialCtx) (program : QuotientProgram) (tcq : QuotientVmTemplateConstants)
    (funs : FunEnv (Bridge.Dialect c g)) (V : VEnv (Bridge.Dialect c g)) (st : EvmState)
    (pc sp x : U256) (p v : Nat)
    (hget_pc : VEnv.get V "q_pc" = some pc)
    (hget_sp : VEnv.get V "q_sp" = some sp)
    (hget_ht : VEnv.get V "q_has_top" = some 0)
    (hget_top : VEnv.get V "q_top" = some x)
    (hget_l0 : VEnv.get V "L_0_MPTR" = some (BitVec.ofNat 256 p))
    (hbyte : (((loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) >>> 248) &&& 0xff) = BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0)
    (hload : loadWord st.memory (BitVec.ofNat 256 p).toNat = BitVec.ofNat 256 v)
    (hl0 : program.memUsage.l0 = true) :
    ExecStmts (Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (GenAst.qArmBodyPushMemToken ctx program tcq)
      (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (("q_ptr", BitVec.ofNat 256 0) :: (VEnv.set (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1))) "q_ptr" (BitVec.ofNat 256 p)) "q_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 v)) "q_has_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 1)) (touchMemory (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (BitVec.ofNat 256 p).toNat 32) .normal := by
  have a1 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (.args []) (.eres (.vals [] st)) :=
    Step.argsNil (D := Bridge.Dialect c g)
  have a2 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (.args []) (.eres (.vals [] st)) :=
    Step.argsNil (D := Bridge.Dialect c g)
  have x3 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (.expr (yvar "q_pc")) (.eres (.vals [pc] st)) :=
    Step.var (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) hget_pc
  have a4 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (.args [yvar "q_pc"]) (.eres (.vals [pc] st)) :=
    Step.argsCons (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (rest := []) (restvals := []) (st1 := st) (v := pc) (st2 := st) a2 x3
  have x5 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (.expr (yop Op.mload [yvar "q_pc"])) (.eres (.vals [loadWord st.memory pc.toNat] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.builtinOk a4 (qbi_mload_at st st.memory pc rfl)
  have w6 :
    (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32).memory = st.memory :=
    Midfall.Yul.Word.touchMemory_memory st pc.toNat 32
  have a7 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (.args [yop Op.mload [yvar "q_pc"]]) (.eres (.vals [loadWord st.memory pc.toNat] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.argsCons (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (rest := []) (restvals := []) (st1 := st) (v := loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) (st2 := touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) a1 x5
  have x8 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (.expr (ylit 0)) (.eres (.vals [BitVec.ofNat 256 0] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.lit (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (l := Literal.number 0)
  have a9 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (.args [ylit 0, yop Op.mload [yvar "q_pc"]]) (.eres (.vals [BitVec.ofNat 256 0, loadWord st.memory pc.toNat] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.argsCons (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (rest := [yop Op.mload [yvar "q_pc"]]) (restvals := [loadWord st.memory pc.toNat]) (st1 := touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (v := BitVec.ofNat 256 0) (st2 := touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) a7 x8
  have x10 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (.expr (yop Op.byte [ylit 0, yop Op.mload [yvar "q_pc"]])) (.eres (.vals [(((loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) >>> 248) &&& 0xff)] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.builtinOk a9 (qbi_byte0 (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (loadWord st.memory pc.toNat))
  have d11 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (.expr (yop Op.byte [ylit 0, yop Op.mload [yvar "q_pc"]])) (.eres (.vals [BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    by rw [<- hbyte]; exact x10
  have s12 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (.stmt (ylet "q_token" (yop Op.byte [ylit 0, yop Op.mload [yvar "q_pc"]]))) (.sres (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) .normal) :=
    Step.letVal d11 rfl
  have g13 :
    VEnv.get (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) "L_0_MPTR" = some (BitVec.ofNat 256 p) :=
    by rw [venvGet_cons_ne V "q_token" "L_0_MPTR" (BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) (by decide : "q_token" ≠ "L_0_MPTR")]; exact hget_l0
  have g14 :
    VEnv.get (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) "q_has_top" = some 0 :=
    by rw [venvGet_cons_ne V "q_token" "q_has_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) (by decide : "q_token" ≠ "q_has_top")]; exact hget_ht
  have g15 :
    VEnv.get (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) "q_pc" = some pc :=
    by rw [venvGet_cons_ne V "q_token" "q_pc" (BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) (by decide : "q_token" ≠ "q_pc")]; exact hget_pc
  have g16 :
    VEnv.get (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) "q_sp" = some sp :=
    by rw [venvGet_cons_ne V "q_token" "q_sp" (BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) (by decide : "q_token" ≠ "q_sp")]; exact hget_sp
  have g17 :
    VEnv.get (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) "q_top" = some x :=
    by rw [venvGet_cons_ne V "q_token" "q_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) (by decide : "q_token" ≠ "q_top")]; exact hget_top
  have g18 :
    VEnv.get (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) "q_token" = some (BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :=
    venvGet_cons_eq V "q_token" (BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0)
  have a19 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (.args []) (.eres (.vals [] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.argsNil (D := Bridge.Dialect c g)
  have x20 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (.expr (ylit 1)) (.eres (.vals [BitVec.ofNat 256 1] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.lit (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (l := Literal.number 1)
  have a21 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (.args [ylit 1]) (.eres (.vals [BitVec.ofNat 256 1] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.argsCons (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (rest := []) (restvals := []) (st1 := touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (v := BitVec.ofNat 256 1) (st2 := touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) a19 x20
  have x22 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (.expr (yvar "q_pc")) (.eres (.vals [pc] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.var (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) g15
  have a23 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (.args [yvar "q_pc", ylit 1]) (.eres (.vals [pc, BitVec.ofNat 256 1] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.argsCons (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (rest := [ylit 1]) (restvals := [BitVec.ofNat 256 1]) (st1 := touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (v := pc) (st2 := touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) a21 x22
  have x24 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (.expr (yop Op.add [yvar "q_pc", ylit 1])) (.eres (.vals [(pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1)] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.builtinOk a23 (qbi_add (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) pc (BitVec.ofNat 256 1))
  have s25 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (.stmt (yset "q_pc" (yop Op.add [yvar "q_pc", ylit 1]))) (.sres (VEnv.set (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1)) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) .normal) :=
    Step.assignVal (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (vars := ["q_pc"]) (vals := [(pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1)]) x24 (by rfl)
  have g26 :
    VEnv.get (VEnv.set (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1)) "L_0_MPTR" = some (BitVec.ofNat 256 p) :=
    venvGet_set_ne (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) "q_pc" "L_0_MPTR" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1) (BitVec.ofNat 256 p) (by decide : "q_pc" ≠ "L_0_MPTR") g13
  have g27 :
    VEnv.get (VEnv.set (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1)) "q_has_top" = some 0 :=
    venvGet_set_ne (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) "q_pc" "q_has_top" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1) 0 (by decide : "q_pc" ≠ "q_has_top") g14
  have g28 :
    VEnv.get (VEnv.set (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1)) "q_sp" = some sp :=
    venvGet_set_ne (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) "q_pc" "q_sp" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1) sp (by decide : "q_pc" ≠ "q_sp") g16
  have g29 :
    VEnv.get (VEnv.set (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1)) "q_token" = some (BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :=
    venvGet_set_ne (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) "q_pc" "q_token" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1) (BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) (by decide : "q_pc" ≠ "q_token") g18
  have g30 :
    VEnv.get (VEnv.set (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1)) "q_top" = some x :=
    venvGet_set_ne (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) "q_pc" "q_top" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1) x (by decide : "q_pc" ≠ "q_top") g17
  have g31 :
    VEnv.get (VEnv.set (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1)) "q_pc" = some (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1) :=
    venvGet_set_same (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1) pc g15
  have x32 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (VEnv.set (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1)) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (.expr (ylit 0)) (.eres (.vals [BitVec.ofNat 256 0] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.lit (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (l := Literal.number 0)
  have s33 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (VEnv.set (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1)) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (.stmt (ylet "q_ptr" (ylit 0))) (.sres (("q_ptr", BitVec.ofNat 256 0) :: (VEnv.set (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1))) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) .normal) :=
    Step.letVal x32 rfl
  have g34 :
    VEnv.get (("q_ptr", BitVec.ofNat 256 0) :: (VEnv.set (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1))) "L_0_MPTR" = some (BitVec.ofNat 256 p) :=
    by rw [venvGet_cons_ne (VEnv.set (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1)) "q_ptr" "L_0_MPTR" (BitVec.ofNat 256 0) (by decide : "q_ptr" ≠ "L_0_MPTR")]; exact g26
  have g35 :
    VEnv.get (("q_ptr", BitVec.ofNat 256 0) :: (VEnv.set (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1))) "q_has_top" = some 0 :=
    by rw [venvGet_cons_ne (VEnv.set (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1)) "q_ptr" "q_has_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 0) (by decide : "q_ptr" ≠ "q_has_top")]; exact g27
  have g36 :
    VEnv.get (("q_ptr", BitVec.ofNat 256 0) :: (VEnv.set (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1))) "q_pc" = some (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1) :=
    by rw [venvGet_cons_ne (VEnv.set (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1)) "q_ptr" "q_pc" (BitVec.ofNat 256 0) (by decide : "q_ptr" ≠ "q_pc")]; exact g31
  have g37 :
    VEnv.get (("q_ptr", BitVec.ofNat 256 0) :: (VEnv.set (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1))) "q_sp" = some sp :=
    by rw [venvGet_cons_ne (VEnv.set (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1)) "q_ptr" "q_sp" (BitVec.ofNat 256 0) (by decide : "q_ptr" ≠ "q_sp")]; exact g28
  have g38 :
    VEnv.get (("q_ptr", BitVec.ofNat 256 0) :: (VEnv.set (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1))) "q_token" = some (BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :=
    by rw [venvGet_cons_ne (VEnv.set (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1)) "q_ptr" "q_token" (BitVec.ofNat 256 0) (by decide : "q_ptr" ≠ "q_token")]; exact g29
  have g39 :
    VEnv.get (("q_ptr", BitVec.ofNat 256 0) :: (VEnv.set (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1))) "q_top" = some x :=
    by rw [venvGet_cons_ne (VEnv.set (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1)) "q_ptr" "q_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 0) (by decide : "q_ptr" ≠ "q_top")]; exact g30
  have g40 :
    VEnv.get (("q_ptr", BitVec.ofNat 256 0) :: (VEnv.set (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1))) "q_ptr" = some (BitVec.ofNat 256 0) :=
    venvGet_cons_eq (VEnv.set (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1)) "q_ptr" (BitVec.ofNat 256 0)
  have x41 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("q_ptr", BitVec.ofNat 256 0) :: (VEnv.set (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1))) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (.expr (yvar "q_token")) (.eres (.vals [BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.var (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) g38

  unfold GenAst.qArmBodyPushMemToken

theorem qArmValue_pushMemToken {c : ExternalCalls} {g : ExternalGas}
    (ctx : PartialCtx) (program : QuotientProgram) (tcq : QuotientVmTemplateConstants)
    (funs : FunEnv (Bridge.Dialect c g)) (pc sp x : U256) (p v : Nat)
    (hl0 : program.memUsage.l0 = true)
    :
    StmtsContract (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (GenAst.qArmBodyPushMemToken ctx program tcq)
      (fun V st => VEnv.get V "q_pc" = some pc ∧
        VEnv.get V "q_sp" = some sp ∧
        VEnv.get V "q_has_top" = some 0 ∧
        VEnv.get V "q_top" = some x ∧
        VEnv.get V "L_0_MPTR" = some (BitVec.ofNat 256 p) ∧
        (((loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) >>> 248) &&& 0xff) = BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0 ∧
        loadWord st.memory (BitVec.ofNat 256 p).toNat = BitVec.ofNat 256 v)
      (fun V st V' st' o => o = .normal 
        ∧ st' = (touchMemory (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (BitVec.ofNat 256 p).toNat 32)
        ∧ V' = (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (("q_ptr", BitVec.ofNat 256 0) :: (VEnv.set (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1))) "q_ptr" (BitVec.ofNat 256 p)) "q_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 v)) "q_has_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 1))) := by
  intro V st hpre
  have hrun := qArmRun_pushMemToken ctx program tcq funs V st pc sp x p v hpre.1 hpre.2.1 hpre.2.2.1 hpre.2.2.2.1 hpre.2.2.2.2.1 hpre.2.2.2.2.2.1 hpre.2.2.2.2.2.2 hl0
  exact ⟨_, _, _, hrun, ⟨rfl, rfl, rfl⟩⟩

theorem qArmValueAll_pushMemToken {c : ExternalCalls} {g : ExternalGas}
    (hc : c.Functional) (hg : g.Functional)
    (ctx : PartialCtx) (program : QuotientProgram) (tcq : QuotientVmTemplateConstants)
    (funs : FunEnv (Bridge.Dialect c g)) (pc sp x : U256) (p v : Nat)
    (hl0 : program.memUsage.l0 = true)
    :
    StmtsContractAll (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (GenAst.qArmBodyPushMemToken ctx program tcq)
      (fun V st => VEnv.get V "q_pc" = some pc ∧
        VEnv.get V "q_sp" = some sp ∧
        VEnv.get V "q_has_top" = some 0 ∧
        VEnv.get V "q_top" = some x ∧
        VEnv.get V "L_0_MPTR" = some (BitVec.ofNat 256 p) ∧
        (((loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) >>> 248) &&& 0xff) = BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0 ∧
        loadWord st.memory (BitVec.ofNat 256 p).toNat = BitVec.ofNat 256 v)
      (fun V st V' st' o => o = .normal 
        ∧ st' = (touchMemory (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (BitVec.ofNat 256 p).toNat 32)
        ∧ V' = (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (("q_ptr", BitVec.ofNat 256 0) :: (VEnv.set (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: V) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 1))) "q_ptr" (BitVec.ofNat 256 p)) "q_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 v)) "q_has_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 1))) :=
  StmtsContractAll.of_det (Bridge.dialect_deterministic hc hg)
    (qArmValue_pushMemToken ctx program tcq funs pc sp x p v hl0)

theorem qArmRun_pushMemTokenOffset {c : ExternalCalls} {g : ExternalGas}
    (ctx : PartialCtx) (program : QuotientProgram) (tcq : QuotientVmTemplateConstants)
    (funs : FunEnv (Bridge.Dialect c g)) (V : VEnv (Bridge.Dialect c g)) (st : EvmState)
    (pc sp x : U256) (ow p v : Nat)
    (hget_pc : VEnv.get V "q_pc" = some pc)
    (hget_sp : VEnv.get V "q_sp" = some sp)
    (hget_ht : VEnv.get V "q_has_top" = some 0)
    (hget_top : VEnv.get V "q_top" = some x)
    (hget_l0 : VEnv.get V "L_0_MPTR" = some (BitVec.ofNat 256 p))
    (hbyte : (((loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) >>> 248) &&& 0xff) = BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0)
    (hoff : (((loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) >>> 216) &&& BitVec.ofNat 256 4294967295) = BitVec.ofNat 256 ow)
    (hload : loadWord st.memory (BitVec.ofNat 256 p + BitVec.ofNat 256 ow).toNat = BitVec.ofNat 256 v)
    (hl0 : program.memUsage.l0 = true) :
    ExecStmts (Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (GenAst.qArmBodyPushMemTokenOffset ctx program tcq)
      (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (("q_ptr", BitVec.ofNat 256 0) :: (VEnv.set (("q_off", BitVec.ofNat 256 ow) :: (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: (("q_word", loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) :: V))) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 5))) "q_ptr" (BitVec.ofNat 256 p + BitVec.ofNat 256 ow)) "q_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 v)) "q_has_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 1)) (touchMemory (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (BitVec.ofNat 256 p + BitVec.ofNat 256 ow).toNat 32) .normal := by
  have a1 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (.args []) (.eres (.vals [] st)) :=
    Step.argsNil (D := Bridge.Dialect c g)
  have x2 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (.expr (yvar "q_pc")) (.eres (.vals [pc] st)) :=
    Step.var (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) hget_pc
  have a3 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (.args [yvar "q_pc"]) (.eres (.vals [pc] st)) :=
    Step.argsCons (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (rest := []) (restvals := []) (st1 := st) (v := pc) (st2 := st) a1 x2
  have x4 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (.expr (yop Op.mload [yvar "q_pc"])) (.eres (.vals [loadWord st.memory pc.toNat] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.builtinOk a3 (qbi_mload_at st st.memory pc rfl)
  have w5 :
    (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32).memory = st.memory :=
    Midfall.Yul.Word.touchMemory_memory st pc.toNat 32
  have s6 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (.stmt (ylet "q_word" (yop Op.mload [yvar "q_pc"]))) (.sres (("q_word", loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) :: V) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) .normal) :=
    Step.letVal x4 rfl
  have g7 :
    VEnv.get (("q_word", loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) :: V) "L_0_MPTR" = some (BitVec.ofNat 256 p) :=
    by rw [venvGet_cons_ne V "q_word" "L_0_MPTR" (loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) (by decide : "q_word" ≠ "L_0_MPTR")]; exact hget_l0
  have g8 :
    VEnv.get (("q_word", loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) :: V) "q_has_top" = some 0 :=
    by rw [venvGet_cons_ne V "q_word" "q_has_top" (loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) (by decide : "q_word" ≠ "q_has_top")]; exact hget_ht
  have g9 :
    VEnv.get (("q_word", loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) :: V) "q_pc" = some pc :=
    by rw [venvGet_cons_ne V "q_word" "q_pc" (loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) (by decide : "q_word" ≠ "q_pc")]; exact hget_pc
  have g10 :
    VEnv.get (("q_word", loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) :: V) "q_sp" = some sp :=
    by rw [venvGet_cons_ne V "q_word" "q_sp" (loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) (by decide : "q_word" ≠ "q_sp")]; exact hget_sp
  have g11 :
    VEnv.get (("q_word", loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) :: V) "q_top" = some x :=
    by rw [venvGet_cons_ne V "q_word" "q_top" (loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) (by decide : "q_word" ≠ "q_top")]; exact hget_top
  have g12 :
    VEnv.get (("q_word", loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) :: V) "q_word" = some (loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) :=
    venvGet_cons_eq V "q_word" (loadWord st.memory pc.toNat)
  have a13 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("q_word", loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) :: V) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (.args []) (.eres (.vals [] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.argsNil (D := Bridge.Dialect c g)
  have x14 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("q_word", loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) :: V) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (.expr (yvar "q_word")) (.eres (.vals [loadWord st.memory pc.toNat] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.var (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) g12
  have a15 :
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("q_word", loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) :: V) (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (.args [yvar "q_word"]) (.eres (.vals [loadWord st.memory pc.toNat] (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32))) :=

theorem qArmValue_pushMemTokenOffset {c : ExternalCalls} {g : ExternalGas}
    (ctx : PartialCtx) (program : QuotientProgram) (tcq : QuotientVmTemplateConstants)
    (funs : FunEnv (Bridge.Dialect c g)) (pc sp x : U256) (ow p v : Nat)
    (hl0 : program.memUsage.l0 = true)
    :
    StmtsContract (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (GenAst.qArmBodyPushMemTokenOffset ctx program tcq)
      (fun V st => VEnv.get V "q_pc" = some pc ∧
        VEnv.get V "q_sp" = some sp ∧
        VEnv.get V "q_has_top" = some 0 ∧
        VEnv.get V "q_top" = some x ∧
        VEnv.get V "L_0_MPTR" = some (BitVec.ofNat 256 p) ∧
        (((loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) >>> 248) &&& 0xff) = BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0 ∧
        (((loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) >>> 216) &&& BitVec.ofNat 256 4294967295) = BitVec.ofNat 256 ow ∧
        loadWord st.memory (BitVec.ofNat 256 p + BitVec.ofNat 256 ow).toNat = BitVec.ofNat 256 v)
      (fun V st V' st' o => o = .normal 
        ∧ st' = (touchMemory (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (BitVec.ofNat 256 p + BitVec.ofNat 256 ow).toNat 32)
        ∧ V' = (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (("q_ptr", BitVec.ofNat 256 0) :: (VEnv.set (("q_off", BitVec.ofNat 256 ow) :: (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: (("q_word", loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) :: V))) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 5))) "q_ptr" (BitVec.ofNat 256 p + BitVec.ofNat 256 ow)) "q_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 v)) "q_has_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 1))) := by
  intro V st hpre
  have hrun := qArmRun_pushMemTokenOffset ctx program tcq funs V st pc sp x ow p v hpre.1 hpre.2.1 hpre.2.2.1 hpre.2.2.2.1 hpre.2.2.2.2.1 hpre.2.2.2.2.2.1 hpre.2.2.2.2.2.2.1 hpre.2.2.2.2.2.2.2 hl0
  exact ⟨_, _, _, hrun, ⟨rfl, rfl, rfl⟩⟩

theorem qArmValueAll_pushMemTokenOffset {c : ExternalCalls} {g : ExternalGas}
    (hc : c.Functional) (hg : g.Functional)
    (ctx : PartialCtx) (program : QuotientProgram) (tcq : QuotientVmTemplateConstants)
    (funs : FunEnv (Bridge.Dialect c g)) (pc sp x : U256) (ow p v : Nat)
    (hl0 : program.memUsage.l0 = true)
    :
    StmtsContractAll (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (GenAst.qArmBodyPushMemTokenOffset ctx program tcq)
      (fun V st => VEnv.get V "q_pc" = some pc ∧
        VEnv.get V "q_sp" = some sp ∧
        VEnv.get V "q_has_top" = some 0 ∧
        VEnv.get V "q_top" = some x ∧
        VEnv.get V "L_0_MPTR" = some (BitVec.ofNat 256 p) ∧
        (((loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) >>> 248) &&& 0xff) = BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0 ∧
        (((loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) >>> 216) &&& BitVec.ofNat 256 4294967295) = BitVec.ofNat 256 ow ∧
        loadWord st.memory (BitVec.ofNat 256 p + BitVec.ofNat 256 ow).toNat = BitVec.ofNat 256 v)
      (fun V st V' st' o => o = .normal 
        ∧ st' = (touchMemory (touchMemory st pc.toNat 32) (BitVec.ofNat 256 p + BitVec.ofNat 256 ow).toNat 32)
        ∧ V' = (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (("q_ptr", BitVec.ofNat 256 0) :: (VEnv.set (("q_off", BitVec.ofNat 256 ow) :: (("q_token", BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.mem.l0) :: (("q_word", loadWord st.memory pc.toNat) :: V))) "q_pc" (pc + BitVec.ofNat 256 5))) "q_ptr" (BitVec.ofNat 256 p + BitVec.ofNat 256 ow)) "q_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 v)) "q_has_top" (BitVec.ofNat 256 1))) :=
  StmtsContractAll.of_det (Bridge.dialect_deterministic hc hg)
    (qArmValue_pushMemTokenOffset ctx program tcq funs pc sp x ow p v hl0)

#print axioms Midfall.LoweringV3.QuotientArms.qArmValue_pushMemToken

/-! ### THE LIMB-LOOP WALL, TAKEN APART (QLIMB): THE REUSABLE LOOP INVARIANT

**WHAT THE FOUR LIMB ARMS NEEDED AND WHY NO FINITE UNROLLING WORKS.** 'LIN7', 'BILIN7_ROW',
'BILIN7_PAIRWISE' and 'MODARITH7' are the four arms that DO have a reference 'qStepValue_X'
('QuotientFrame' section B) and are therefore the same deliverable as QMEM's eleven and QTOKEN's two -
but the emitted 'yfor' iterates 'tcq.limbCount', a SYMBOLIC field of 'QuotientVmTemplateConstants'
('VmTypes.lean:987'). QMEM and QTOKEN both reached this wall independently and both said the same
thing. They were right: no numeral unrolling proves them. What they need is an INVARIANT over
'Step.loopStep'/'Step.loopDone', and that invariant is what this section lands. It is also exactly
what the three count-prefixed arms (RUN x2, AFFINE_SUM) will need, so it is reusable infrastructure
and not four rows.

'qLimbWalk' is the rule: 'Inv m V st' is the loop-head predicate after 'm' completed iterations,
'hiter' supplies ONE iteration (guard true, body block, post block, 'Inv (m+1)'), 'hexit' says the
guard is false at 'm = N'. The induction is on the iterations REMAINING ('m + f = N'), which is what
makes a symbolic 'N' admissible - nothing is ever unrolled to a numeral. 'cond', 'post' and 'body'
are PARAMETERS, not fixed syntax, which is what makes the one lemma serve four arms: only 'Inv',
'hiter' and 'hexit' differ per arm. It is stated on the raw 'Step' judgment, so it composes with
'Step.forLoop' directly, and it checks at '[propext, Quot.sound]' with ZERO sorries.

'lin7_iter' is the first consumer: the emitted LIN7 body block, ONE iteration, derived from a
symbolic loop-head environment to the symbolic next one. It is the per-iteration effect the four arms
share, and it too is sorried-free at '[propext, Quot.sound]'.

**THE THREE THINGS THAT BITE, MEASURED HERE.**
* 'Step.argsCons' evaluates the REST of the argument list BEFORE its head ('BigStep.lean:209-212':
  the rest runs 'st -> st1' and the head runs 'st1 -> st2'). So the emitted
  'mulmod(mload(caddr), mload(q_ptr), r)' touches 'q_ptr' FIRST and the constant slot SECOND - the
  reverse of the source order. Getting this backwards is not reported where it is wrong; it surfaces
  three steps later as a state mismatch.
* A limb body 'let's three locals and then ASSIGNS two existing registers, so 'Step.block's 'restore'
  drops exactly the three locals and keeps both writes. 'restore_c3_set2' is that fact, with
  'venvSet_c3' pushing each assignment out past the three conses; 'venvSet_set_same' then collapses
  the per-iteration 'q_pc' write chain.
* 'Step.lit' ascribes 'D.litValue (Literal.number n)'. With a NON-ATOMIC state argument the elaborator
  will not unfold the '@[reducible]' 'Bridge.Dialect' to see that it IS 'BitVec.ofNat 256 n', and the
  ascription fails with a bare 'Type mismatch'. 'qlit' pays that unfolding ONCE, by cast, at
  '[propext]'; every literal in 'lin7_iter' goes through it.

**WHAT IS STILL NOT LANDED, AND THE EXACT STATE OF IT.** The four arm triples. 'lin7_iter' gives the
per-iteration step; each arm needs 'qLimbWalk' instantiated at its own 'Inv' - for LIN7, loop-head
environment '(q_i, m) :: (q_acc, (lin7Walk cm m s0)).2.1 :: VEnv.set V "q_pc" (lin7Walk cm m s0).1'
and state '(lin7Walk cm m s0).2.2' - then 'Step.forLoop' and the two trailing assignments. One
further fact is needed and is NOT free: the guard 'lt(q_i, ylit tcq.limbCount)' compares WORDS, so the
walk runs '(BitVec.ofNat 256 tcq.limbCount).toNat' times, and that equals 'tcq.limbCount' only under
'tcq.limbCount < 2 ^ 256'. That hypothesis is a template-constants side condition, not a row fact; it
belongs on the arm, and no row, 'ValidV3', bundle or the root gains anything (RULES).

**THE EMITTED/REFERENCE COUNT MISMATCH - REPORTED, NOT EDITED.** 'limbLinearForm' and its three
siblings fold over the GLOBAL 'quotientVmLimbs' ('VmReference.lean:576-589'), while the emitted arm
folds over 'tcq.limbCount'. The two agree exactly when 'tcq.limbCount = quotientVmLimbs', which the
KAT 'limbCounts_crossCheckAccumulatorLimbs_ok' pins for the DEFAULT table only. So the middle theorem
of each limb triple - the 'w..._eq_qvmFrX' tie - is only statable with that equation as a hypothesis.
That is a real gap between the emitter (parameterised) and the reference (not); it is recorded here
rather than papered over, and 'VmReference.lean' is read-only from here.

**GENERATOR: NOT USED, AND WHY.** 'formal/scripts/qmem/' models an arm as a STRAIGHT-LINE statement
list it symbolically executes; a loop-driven arm has no such model - the derivation is an induction
with a symbolic index, not a list of steps. 'lin7_iter' is therefore hand-written. No generator file
was changed.

-/

theorem QSHR232 {c : ExternalCalls} {g : ExternalGas} (st : EvmState) (a : U256) :
    (Bridge.Dialect c g).Builtin Op.shr [BitVec.ofNat 256 232, a] st (.ok [a >>> 232] st) := rfl

theorem QSHL5 {c : ExternalCalls} {g : ExternalGas} (st : EvmState) (v : U256) :
    (Bridge.Dialect c g).Builtin Op.shl [BitVec.ofNat 256 5, v] st (.ok [v <<< 5] st) := rfl

theorem venvSet_cons_eq {D : Dialect} (V : VEnv D) (x : Ident) (w v : D.Value) :
    VEnv.set ((x, w) :: V) x v = (x, v) :: V := by
  simp [VEnv.set]

theorem venvGet_c3 {D : Dialect} (V : VEnv D) (a b c : Ident) (va vb vc v : D.Value) (x : Ident)
    (ha : a ≠ x) (hb : b ≠ x) (hc : c ≠ x) (h : VEnv.get V x = some v) :
    VEnv.get ((c, vc) :: (b, vb) :: (a, va) :: V) x = some v := by
  rw [venvGet_cons_ne ((b, vb) :: (a, va) :: V) c x vc hc,
      venvGet_cons_ne ((a, va) :: V) b x vb hb,
      venvGet_cons_ne V a x va ha]
  exact h

theorem venvSet_c3 {D : Dialect} (V : VEnv D) (a b c : Ident) (va vb vc v : D.Value) (x : Ident)
    (ha : a ≠ x) (hb : b ≠ x) (hc : c ≠ x) :
    VEnv.set ((c, vc) :: (b, vb) :: (a, va) :: V) x v
      = (c, vc) :: (b, vb) :: (a, va) :: VEnv.set V x v := by
  rw [venvSet_cons_ne ((b, vb) :: (a, va) :: V) c x vc v hc,
      venvSet_cons_ne ((a, va) :: V) b x vb v hb,
      venvSet_cons_ne V a x va v ha]

theorem drop3 {a : Type} (x y z : a) (V : List a) : List.drop 3 (x :: y :: z :: V) = V := by
  rw [List.drop_succ_cons, List.drop_succ_cons, List.drop_succ_cons, List.drop_zero]

/-- **THE 'restore' FACT A LIMB LOOP BODY NEEDS.** The three locals a limb body 'let's are dropped by
    'Step.block's 'restore'; the two register writes it makes IN PLACE survive. -/
theorem restore_c3_set2 {D : Dialect} (V : VEnv D) (a b c x y : Ident)
    (va vb vc vx vy : D.Value) :
    restore V ((c, vc) :: (b, vb) :: (a, va) :: VEnv.set (VEnv.set V x vx) y vy)
      = VEnv.set (VEnv.set V x vx) y vy := by
  have hlen : ((c, vc) :: (b, vb) :: (a, va) :: VEnv.set (VEnv.set V x vx) y vy).length
      - V.length = 3 := by
    rw [List.length_cons, List.length_cons, List.length_cons, venvSet_length, venvSet_length]
    omega
  show ((c, vc) :: (b, vb) :: (a, va) :: VEnv.set (VEnv.set V x vx) y vy).drop _ = _
  rw [hlen, drop3]

/-- Writing a register the value it ALREADY holds is the identity. -/
theorem venvSet_same_of_get {D : Dialect} : ∀ (V : VEnv D) (x : Ident) (v : D.Value),
    VEnv.get V x = some v → VEnv.set V x v = V
  | [], x, v, h => by cases h
  | ((y, w) :: rest), x, v, h => by
      by_cases hyx : y = x
      · subst hyx
        have h1 : VEnv.get ((y, w) :: rest) y = some w := venvGet_cons_eq rest y w
        rw [h1] at h
        have hwv : w = v := Option.some.inj h
        subst hwv
        simp [VEnv.set]
      · rw [venvSet_cons_ne rest y x w v hyx]
        have h2 : VEnv.get ((y, w) :: rest) x = VEnv.get rest x :=
          venvGet_cons_ne rest y x w hyx
        rw [h2] at h
        rw [venvSet_same_of_get rest x v h]

/-- The LAST write to a register wins. -/
theorem venvSet_set_same {D : Dialect} : ∀ (V : VEnv D) (x : Ident) (v w : D.Value),
    VEnv.set (VEnv.set V x v) x w = VEnv.set V x w
  | [], x, v, w => rfl
  | ((y, u) :: rest), x, v, w => by
      by_cases hyx : y = x
      · subst hyx
        have h1 : VEnv.set ((y, u) :: rest) y v = (y, v) :: rest := by simp [VEnv.set]
        have h2 : VEnv.set ((y, v) :: rest) y w = (y, w) :: rest := by simp [VEnv.set]
        have h3 : VEnv.set ((y, u) :: rest) y w = (y, w) :: rest := by simp [VEnv.set]
        rw [h1, h2, h3]
      · have h1 : VEnv.set ((y, u) :: rest) x v = (y, u) :: VEnv.set rest x v :=
            venvSet_cons_ne rest y x u v hyx
        rw [h1, venvSet_cons_ne (VEnv.set rest x v) y x u w hyx,
            venvSet_cons_ne rest y x u w hyx, venvSet_set_same rest x v w]

/-- **THE REUSABLE LOOP INVARIANT FOR A COUNT-DRIVEN LIMB WALK.** -/
theorem qLimbWalk {D : Dialect} [DecidableEq D.Value] (funs : FunEnv D)
    (cond : Expr D.Op) (post body : Block D.Op)
    (N : Nat) (Inv : Nat → VEnv D → D.State → Prop)
    (hexit : ∀ V st, Inv N V st →
        ∃ cv st', Step D funs V st (.expr cond) (.eres (.vals [cv] st')) ∧ cv = D.zero ∧
          Inv N V st')
    (hiter : ∀ m V st, m < N → Inv m V st →
        ∃ cv stc Vb stb Vp stp,
          Step D funs V st (.expr cond) (.eres (.vals [cv] stc)) ∧ cv ≠ D.zero ∧
          Step D funs V stc (.stmt (.block body)) (.sres Vb stb .normal) ∧
          Step D funs Vb stb (.stmt (.block post)) (.sres Vp stp .normal) ∧
          Inv (m + 1) Vp stp) :
    ∀ V st, Inv 0 V st →
      ∃ V' st', Step D funs V st (.loop cond post body) (.sres V' st' .normal) ∧ Inv N V' st' := by
  have key : ∀ f m, m + f = N → ∀ V st, Inv m V st →
      ∃ V' st', Step D funs V st (.loop cond post body) (.sres V' st' .normal) ∧ Inv N V' st' := by
    intro f
    induction f with
    | zero =>
        intro m hf V st hInv
        have hm : m = N := by omega
        subst hm
        obtain ⟨cv, st', hc, hz, hInv'⟩ := hexit V st hInv
        exact ⟨V, st', Step.loopDone hc hz, hInv'⟩
    | succ f ih =>
        intro m hf V st hInv
        have hlt : m < N := by omega
        obtain ⟨cv, stc, Vb, stb, Vp, stp, hc, hnz, hbody, hpost, hInv'⟩ := hiter m V st hlt hInv
        obtain ⟨V', st', hloop, hfin⟩ := ih (m + 1) (by omega) Vp stp hInv'
        exact ⟨V', st', Step.loopStep hc hnz hbody (Or.inl rfl) hpost hloop, hfin⟩
  intro V st hInv
  exact key N 0 (by omega) V st hInv

#print axioms qLimbWalk
#cone_sorries qLimbWalk

/-- A `Step.lit` whose value is spelled as the machine WORD rather than as the dialect projection.
    `Step.lit` ascribes `D.litValue (Literal.number n)`, and with a non-atomic state argument the
    elaborator will not unfold the `@[reducible]` `Bridge.Dialect` to see that it IS
    `BitVec.ofNat 256 n`; the cast does the unfolding once, at `[propext]`. -/
theorem qlit {c : ExternalCalls} {g : ExternalGas} (funs : FunEnv (Bridge.Dialect c g))
    (V : VEnv (Bridge.Dialect c g)) (st : EvmState) (n : Nat) :
    Step (Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (.expr (ylit n)) (.eres (.vals [BitVec.ofNat 256 n] st)) := by
  have h : Step (Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (.expr (ylit n))
      (.eres (.vals [(Bridge.Dialect c g).litValue (Literal.number n)] st)) :=
    Step.lit (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (l := Literal.number n)
  exact (qDialect_litValue_number n) ▸ h

theorem lin7_iter {c : ExternalCalls} {g : ExternalGas} (funs : FunEnv (Bridge.Dialect c g))
    (V : VEnv (Bridge.Dialect c g)) (st : EvmState) (pcv accv cm : U256)
    (hpc : VEnv.get V "q_pc" = some pcv)
    (hacc : VEnv.get V "q_acc" = some accv)
    (hcm : VEnv.get V "q_const_mptr" = some cm)
    (hr : VEnv.get V "r" = some frModulusWord) :
    let w := loadWord st.memory pcv.toNat
    let slot := (w >>> 248) &&& 0xff
    let ptr := (w >>> 232) &&& 65535
    let cptr := cm + (slot <<< 5)
    Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (.stmts [(ylet "q_word" (yop EVM.Op.mload [(yvar "q_pc")])), (ylet "qconst" (yop EVM.Op.byte [(ylit 0), (yvar "q_word")])), (ylet "q_ptr" (yop EVM.Op.and [(yop EVM.Op.shr [(ylit 232), (yvar "q_word")]), (ylit 65535)])), (yset "q_pc" (yop EVM.Op.add [(yvar "q_pc"), (ylit 3)])), (yset "q_acc" (yop EVM.Op.addmod [(yvar "q_acc"), (yop EVM.Op.mulmod [(yop EVM.Op.mload [(yop EVM.Op.add [(yvar "q_const_mptr"), (yop EVM.Op.shl [(ylit 5), (yvar "qconst")])])]), (yop EVM.Op.mload [(yvar "q_ptr")]), (yvar "r")]), (yvar "r")]))])
        (.sres (VEnv.set (VEnv.set (("q_ptr", ptr) :: ("qconst", slot) :: ("q_word", w) :: V) "q_pc" (pcv + BitVec.ofNat 256 3)) "q_acc" (wAddmod accv (wMulmod (loadWord st.memory cptr.toNat) (loadWord st.memory ptr.toNat)))) (touchMemory (touchMemory (touchMemory st pcv.toNat 32) ptr.toNat 32) cptr.toNat 32) .normal) := by
  intro w slot ptr cptr
  have b1 : Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (.expr (yvar "q_pc")) (.eres (.vals [pcv] st)) :=
    Step.var (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) hpc
  have c1 : Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (.args [yvar "q_pc"]) (.eres (.vals [pcv] st)) :=
    Step.argsCons (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (rest := []) (restvals := []) (st1 := st) (v := pcv) (st2 := st) (Step.argsNil (D := Bridge.Dialect c g)) b1
  have d1 : Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (.expr (yop EVM.Op.mload [(yvar "q_pc")])) (.eres (.vals [w] (touchMemory st pcv.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.builtinOk (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) c1 (qbi_mload_at st st.memory pcv rfl)
  have e1 : Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs V st (.stmt (ylet "q_word" (yop EVM.Op.mload [(yvar "q_pc")]))) (.sres (("q_word", w) :: V) (touchMemory st pcv.toNat 32) .normal) :=
    Step.letVal (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) d1 rfl
  have f2 : Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("q_word", w) :: V) (touchMemory st pcv.toNat 32) (.expr (ylit 0)) (.eres (.vals [BitVec.ofNat 256 0] (touchMemory st pcv.toNat 32))) :=
    qlit funs (("q_word", w) :: V) (touchMemory st pcv.toNat 32) 0
  have g2 : Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("q_word", w) :: V) (touchMemory st pcv.toNat 32) (.expr (yvar "q_word")) (.eres (.vals [w] (touchMemory st pcv.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.var (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (venvGet_cons_eq V "q_word" w)
  have h2 : Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("q_word", w) :: V) (touchMemory st pcv.toNat 32) (.args [yvar "q_word"]) (.eres (.vals [w] (touchMemory st pcv.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.argsCons (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (rest := []) (restvals := []) (st1 := (touchMemory st pcv.toNat 32)) (v := w) (st2 := (touchMemory st pcv.toNat 32)) (Step.argsNil (D := Bridge.Dialect c g)) g2
  have i2 : Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("q_word", w) :: V) (touchMemory st pcv.toNat 32) (.args [ylit 0, yvar "q_word"])
      (.eres (.vals [BitVec.ofNat 256 0, w] (touchMemory st pcv.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.argsCons (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (rest := [yvar "q_word"]) (restvals := [w]) (st1 := (touchMemory st pcv.toNat 32))
      (v := BitVec.ofNat 256 0) (st2 := (touchMemory st pcv.toNat 32)) h2 f2
  have j2 : Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("q_word", w) :: V) (touchMemory st pcv.toNat 32) (.expr (yop EVM.Op.byte [(ylit 0), (yvar "q_word")])) (.eres (.vals [slot] (touchMemory st pcv.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.builtinOk (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) i2 (qbi_byte0 (touchMemory st pcv.toNat 32) w)
  have k2 : Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("q_word", w) :: V) (touchMemory st pcv.toNat 32) (.stmt (ylet "qconst" (yop EVM.Op.byte [(ylit 0), (yvar "q_word")]))) (.sres (("qconst", slot) :: ("q_word", w) :: V) (touchMemory st pcv.toNat 32) .normal) :=
    Step.letVal (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) j2 rfl
  have l3 : Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("qconst", slot) :: ("q_word", w) :: V) (touchMemory st pcv.toNat 32) (.expr (ylit 232)) (.eres (.vals [BitVec.ofNat 256 232] (touchMemory st pcv.toNat 32))) :=
    qlit funs (("qconst", slot) :: ("q_word", w) :: V) (touchMemory st pcv.toNat 32) 232
  have m3 : Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("qconst", slot) :: ("q_word", w) :: V) (touchMemory st pcv.toNat 32) (.expr (yvar "q_word")) (.eres (.vals [w] (touchMemory st pcv.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.var (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (venvGet_cons_ne (("q_word", w) :: V) "qconst" "q_word" slot
      (by decide : ("qconst" : String) ≠ "q_word"))
  have n3 : Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("qconst", slot) :: ("q_word", w) :: V) (touchMemory st pcv.toNat 32) (.args [yvar "q_word"])
      (.eres (.vals [w] (touchMemory st pcv.toNat 32))) :=
    Step.argsCons (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (rest := []) (restvals := []) (st1 := (touchMemory st pcv.toNat 32)) (v := w)
      (st2 := (touchMemory st pcv.toNat 32)) (Step.argsNil (D := Bridge.Dialect c g)) m3
  have o3 : Step (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) funs (("qconst", slot) :: ("q_word", w) :: V) (touchMemory st pcv.toNat 32) (.args [ylit 232, yvar "q_word"])
      (.eres (.vals [BitVec.ofNat 256 232, w] (touchMemory st pcv.toNat 32))) :=
